<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-48734373" data-id="48734373" data-tags="AI|Privacy" data-score="2395" data-comments="731" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48734373" data-time="1782834264">
<a class="thumb" href="https://thereallo.dev/blog/claude-code-prompt-steganography" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=thereallo.dev&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>thereallo.dev</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thereallo.dev/blog/claude-code-prompt-steganography" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code is steganographically marking requests</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 以隐写方式标记请求</span></a><span class="dom">thereallo.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate how Claude Code can embed hidden signals in prompts, enabling steganographic tagging of requests.</span><span class='zh'>研究者展示了 Claude Code 如何在提示中嵌入隐写信号，给请求标记隐藏标识。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate how Claude Code can embed hidden signals in prompts, enabling steganographic tagging of requests. This raises questions about model privacy, misuse, and provenance in AI-assisted workflows. The finding highlights the need for robust controls around prompt encoding.</span><span class='zh'>研究者展示了 Claude Code 如何在提示中嵌入隐写信号，给请求标记隐藏标识。这引发对模型隐私、滥用和工作流可追溯性的担忧，凸显了对提示编码的鲁棒控制的必要性。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>2395</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48734373' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>731</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-48755965" data-id="48755965" data-tags="Security" data-score="1665" data-comments="714" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48755965" data-time="1782961215">
<a class="thumb" href="https://f-droid.org/2026/07/01/adv-malware.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=f-droid.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>f-droid.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://f-droid.org/2026/07/01/adv-malware.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Android Developer Verification: Threat masquerading as protection</span><span class='zh'>Android 开发者验证：伪装成保护的威胁</span></a><span class="dom">f-droid.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Android developer verification can be abused by threat actors who disguise malware as protective or security tools.</span><span class='zh'>Android 开发者验证可能被威胁者利用，伪装成保护性工具的恶意软件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Android developer verification can be abused by threat actors who disguise malware as protective or security tools. Such tactics exploit trust signals around security features to mislead users or bypass vetting, undermining platform defenses. The takeaway is a push for tighter vetting, clearer indicators of app behavior, and user skepticism when apps claim to protect you.</span><span class='zh'>Android 开发者验证可能被威胁者利用，伪装成保护性工具的恶意软件。此类手段利用安全功能的信任信号误导用户或规避审核，削弱平台防护。要点在于加强审核、提供更清晰的应用行为指示，并提高用户在遇到自称保护工具的应用时的警惕。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>1665</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48755965' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>714</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-48772388" data-id="48772388" data-tags="Design|Business" data-score="1297" data-comments="387" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48772388" data-time="1783067012">

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/news/claude-sonnet-5" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Sonnet 5</span><span class='zh'>Claude Sonnet 5</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://quesma.com/blog/qwen-36-is-awesome/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Qwen 3.6 27B is the sweet spot for local development</span><span class='zh'>Qwen 3.6 27B：本地开发的最佳平衡点</span></a><span class="dom">quesma.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Qwen 3.6 27B hits a practical sweet spot for local AI development.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen 3.6 27B 在本地 AI 开发中找到了实用的平衡点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Qwen 3.6 27B hits a practical sweet spot for local AI development. It provides a capable, smaller model that runs on modest GPUs, easing offline prototyping and testing without cloud latency. For developers, this balance between performance and footprint matters as it accelerates iteration cycles.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen 3.6 27B 在本地 AI 开发中找到了实用的平衡点。它具备足够能力、体积适中，能在普通显卡上运行，降低离线原型与测试对云端延迟的依赖。对开发者而言，这种性能与资源的权衡能显著提升迭代速度。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://semgrep.dev/blog/2026/we-have-mythos-at-home-glm-52-beats-claude-in-our-cyber-benchmarks/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GLM 5.2 beats Claude in our benchmarks</span><span class='zh'>GLM 5.2 在基准测试中击败 Claude</span></a><span class="dom">semgrep.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GLM 5.2 beat Claude in our benchmarks, showing that newer open models are narrowing the gap with top proprietary rivals.</span><span class='zh'>GLM 5.2 在我们的基准测试中击败 Claude，显示较新的开源模型正在缩小与顶级专有模型的差距。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GLM 5.2 beat Claude in our benchmarks, showing that newer open models are narrowing the gap with top proprietary rivals. The result reinforces that cost, licensing, and ecosystem maturity can influence real-world decisions as AI hardware and tooling evolve.</span><span class='zh'>GLM 5.2 在我们的基准测试中击败 Claude，显示较新的开源模型正在缩小与顶级专有模型的差距。该结果强调，在推理硬件和工具集持续演进的背景下，成本、许可与生态系统成熟度可能成为实际选型的关键因素。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>1084</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48709670' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>503</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-48714529" data-id="48714529" data-tags="Privacy|AI" data-score="989" data-comments="608" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48714529" data-time="1782704527">
<a class="thumb" href="https://nonogra.ph/age-verification-is-just-a-precursor-to-attribution-of-speech-06-29-2026" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=nonogra.ph&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>nonogra.ph</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://nonogra.ph/age-verification-is-just-a-precursor-to-attribution-of-speech-06-29-2026" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Age verification is just a precursor to automated attribution of speech</span><span class='zh'>年龄验证只是自动化语音归因的前提</span></a><span class="dom">nonogra.ph</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that age verification schemes may simply enable automatic attribution of who said what in speech.</span><span class='zh'>文中指出，年龄验证可能只是实现对语音进行自动归因的前提条件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that age verification schemes may simply enable automatic attribution of who said what in speech. As identity becomes tied to voice data, safeguards around privacy, consent, and misuse become critical, with implications for policy and AI system design.</span><span class='zh'>文中指出，年龄验证可能只是实现对语音进行自动归因的前提条件。当声音数据与身份绑定时，隐私、同意和滥用防护就变得尤为关键，对政策和 AI 系统设计都会产生影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://danunparsed.com/p/hackerrank-open-source-ats" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>HackerRank open sourced its ATS. My resume scored 90/100. Oh wait 74. No – 88</span><span class='zh'>HackerRank 开源其 ATS，简历评分波动引发关注</span></a><span class="dom">danunparsed.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HackerRank released its ATS as open source, exposing how resumes are scored in practice.</span><span class='zh'>HackerRank 将其 ATS 开源，使人们能审视简历评分的全过程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HackerRank released its ATS as open source, exposing how resumes are scored in practice. A single resume yielded a string of conflicting scores (90, then 74, then 88), illustrating instability and potential bias in automated screening. The takeaway: hiring AI needs better transparency, reproducibility, and fairness checks.</span><span class='zh'>HackerRank 将其 ATS 开源，使人们能审视简历评分的全过程。一次测试的简历竟得到 90、74、88 等不同分数，凸显自动筛选的不稳定性和潜在偏见。要点是招聘 AI 需加强透明性、可重复性与公平性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Department of Commerce has eased export controls on Claude Fable 5 and Mythos 5, enabling broader international distribution.</span><span class='zh'>商务部放松了对 Claude Fable 5 和 Mythos 5 的出口管制，使其在全球范围内的分发更为广泛。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Department of Commerce has eased export controls on Claude Fable 5 and Mythos 5, enabling broader international distribution. The move could accelerate global AI deployment and competition, while inviting regulatory and security considerations.</span><span class='zh'>商务部放松了对 Claude Fable 5 和 Mythos 5 的出口管制，使其在全球范围内的分发更为广泛。这一举措可能加速全球 AI 的部署与竞争，同时带来监管与安全方面的考量。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.hunton.com/privacy-and-cybersecurity-law-blog/virginia-bans-sale-of-geolocation-data" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Virginia bans sale of precise geolocation data</span><span class='zh'>弗吉尼亚州禁止出售地理位置数据</span></a><span class="dom">hunton.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Virginia&#x27;s new privacy rule bans the sale of geolocation data collected from devices.</span><span class='zh'>弗吉尼亚州出台隐私新规，禁止出售设备收集的地理位置信息数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Virginia&#x27;s new privacy rule bans the sale of geolocation data collected from devices. The move tightens location data markets and will impact marketers, data brokers, and app developers who rely on location signals. It also heightens regulatory fragmentation as more states consider similar restrictions.</span><span class='zh'>弗吉尼亚州出台隐私新规，禁止出售设备收集的地理位置信息数据。此举收紧了地理位置信息市场，影响依赖位置信号的广告商、数据经纪人与应用开发者。同时，这也可能推动其他州采取类似限制，加剧合规碎片化。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>941</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48767347' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>137</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48747304" data-id="48747304" data-tags="Science" data-score="940" data-comments="295" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48747304" data-time="1782915652">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.quantamagazine.org/for-the-first-time-a-cell-built-from-scratch-grows-and-divides-20260701/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/07/SyntheticLife-cr-Ada-Zejun-Shen-social.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='quantamagazine.org' data-c='#10b981' data-tag='Science'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">11</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.quantamagazine.org/for-the-first-time-a-cell-built-from-scratch-grows-and-divides-20260701/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>For first time, a cell built from scratch grows and divides</span><span class='zh'>首次从零构建的细胞能够生长并分裂</span></a><span class="dom">quantamagazine.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers have built a cell from scratch that can grow and divide, marking a milestone in bottom-up synthetic biology.</span><span class='zh'>研究者首次从零构建出可生长和分裂的细胞，标志着自下而上的合成生物学里程碑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers have built a cell from scratch that can grow and divide, marking a milestone in bottom-up synthetic biology. By assembling functional cellular life from individual components, the work advances the dream of programmable biology and could unlock new biotechnologies and origins-of-life insights. Yet, challenges around stability, safety, and control remain as researchers push toward more complex designs.</span><span class='zh'>研究者首次从零构建出可生长和分裂的细胞，标志着自下而上的合成生物学里程碑。通过从单个组件组装功能性细胞生命，这项工作推动了可编程生物学的实现，并可能为生物技术和起源研究带来新机遇。当然，稳定性、安全性与可控性仍是未来进一步复杂化设计时需要面对的挑战。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Science' style='--c:#10b981'>Science</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>940</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48747304' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>295</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48716902" data-id="48716902" data-tags="Politics|Legal" data-score="897" data-comments="126" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48716902" data-time="1782725288">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.pragmaticengineer.com/pollen-tried-to-remove-my-article-about-callum-negus-fancey-and-google-is-assisting-to-it/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pollen tried to remove my article and Google is assisting with it</span><span class='zh'>Pollen 试图删除我的文章，Google 正在协助</span></a><span class="dom">blog.pragmaticengineer.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An investigative post claims Pollen tried to suppress a journalist&#x27;s article about Callum Negus-Fancey, with Google allegedly aiding the effort.</span><span class='zh'>据称，Pollen 试图删除一篇关于 Callum Negus-Fancey 的文章，谷歌据称在协助此事。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An investigative post claims Pollen tried to suppress a journalist&#x27;s article about Callum Negus-Fancey, with Google allegedly aiding the effort. The episode highlights how platform power can influence publication visibility and the fragility of independent reporting in high-stakes tech stories. It also raises questions about transparency and due process in takedown cooperation between platforms and search engines.</span><span class='zh'>据称，Pollen 试图删除一篇关于 Callum Negus-Fancey 的文章，谷歌据称在协助此事。此事揭示了平台权力对内容可见性和独立报道的脆弱性，引发对透明度与程序正义的担忧。事件也凸显了在高风险科技报道中协作取证过程的复杂性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.mikebowler.ca/2026/07/03/co2-and-decision-making/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The bottleneck might be the air in the room</span><span class='zh'>瓶颈可能在房间里的空气</span></a><span class="dom">blog.mikebowler.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CO2 buildup in poorly ventilated spaces can impair decision-making and cognitive performance, setting a hidden bottleneck for teams.</span><span class='zh'>在密闭空间里二氧化碳积聚会削弱决策能力和认知表现，成为团队的潜在瓶颈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CO2 buildup in poorly ventilated spaces can impair decision-making and cognitive performance, setting a hidden bottleneck for teams. The post argues that improving ventilation and air quality is a cost-effective way to boost focus and judgment in meetings and workplaces.</span><span class='zh'>在密闭空间里二氧化碳积聚会削弱决策能力和认知表现，成为团队的潜在瓶颈。文章强调通过改善通风和空气质量是提升专注力与判断力的成本效益办法，尤其在会议和工作场所。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.playstation.com/2026/07/01/physical-disc-production-ending-in-january-2028-for-new-games-releasing-on-playstation-consoles/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Physical disc production ending in Jan 2028 for new games on PlayStation</span><span class='zh'>PlayStation 将于 2028 年 1 月停止新作实体光盘生产</span></a><span class="dom">blog.playstation.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sony will end physical disc production for new PlayStation releases in January 2028, accelerating its digital-first strategy.</span><span class='zh'>索尼将于 2028 年 1 月停止新作实体光盘的生产，进一步推动数字化优先策略。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sony will end physical disc production for new PlayStation releases in January 2028, accelerating its digital-first strategy. The move reduces manufacturing and distribution costs but may affect collectors, retailers, and regions with stronger physical markets. It signals a lasting shift in how console games are distributed and consumed.</span><span class='zh'>索尼将于 2028 年 1 月停止新作实体光盘的生产，进一步推动数字化优先策略。此举降低了制造与发行成本，但可能影响收藏者、零售商以及实体市场较强的地区，预示着主机游戏发行与消费方式的长期转变。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.patrick-breyer.de/en/double-threat-to-private-communications-undemocratic-chat-control-backroom-deals-and-imminent-concessions-spark-relaunch-of-fightchatcontrol-eu/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>EU to legislate about Chat Control behind closed doors</span><span class='zh'>欧盟在幕后推动聊天监控立法</span></a><span class="dom">patrick-breyer.de</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reports indicate the EU plans to legislate on Chat Control behind closed doors, raising concerns about transparency and civil liberties.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，欧盟正在幕后推动“聊天控制”相关立法，引发对透明度和公民自由的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reports indicate the EU plans to legislate on Chat Control behind closed doors, raising concerns about transparency and civil liberties. The move signals a push to regulate private communications and could set norms that influence global tech policy.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，欧盟正在幕后推动“聊天控制”相关立法，引发对透明度和公民自由的担忧。此举表明欧洲正推动对私人通信的规制，可能对全球科技政策产生重要影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://wangcong.org/2026-06-30-why-i-stopped-arguing-with-people.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Most arguments are about ego, not ideas</span><span class='zh'>大多數爭論源於自尊心，而非觀點</span></a><span class="dom">wangcong.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Most online disagreements stem from ego rather than substantive ideas.</span><span class='zh'>大多數在線爭論源於自尊心，而非實質性觀點。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Most online disagreements stem from ego rather than substantive ideas. The piece argues for stepping away from heated exchanges and focusing on listening and shared goals, which can make discussions more productive and informative for tech communities.</span><span class='zh'>大多數在線爭論源於自尊心，而非實質性觀點。文章倡導在情緒升溫時停止爭辯，專注傾聽並明確共同目標，讓技術社群的討論更高效、具啟發性。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://waag.org/en/article/european-digital-id-wallets-are-gift-google-and-apple/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cms.waag.org/sites/waag/files/styles/1200x630/public/2026-06/scherm-afbeelding_2026-06-23_om_10.31.35.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='waag.org' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">19</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://waag.org/en/article/european-digital-id-wallets-are-gift-google-and-apple/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>European digital ID wallets rely on safety services of Google and Apple</span><span class='zh'>欧洲数字身份证钱包依赖谷歌和苹果的安全服务</span></a><span class="dom">waag.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>European digital ID wallets rely on the safety services from Google and Apple, tying cross-border identity tech to US platform ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲数字身份钱包在安全服务上高度依赖谷歌与苹果，依托成熟的安全生态提升跨境互通。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>European digital ID wallets rely on the safety services from Google and Apple, tying cross-border identity tech to US platform ecosystems. While this brings strong security and interoperability, it raises questions about data sovereignty, governance, and vendor dependence as Europe pushes self-sovereign identity models.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲数字身份钱包在安全服务上高度依赖谷歌与苹果，依托成熟的安全生态提升跨境互通。与此同时，这种依赖也引发数据主权、治理和对厂商依赖的担忧，或影响未来的自我主权身份框架的推进。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>695</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730729' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>299</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48711981" data-id="48711981" data-tags="Politics|Legal" data-score="681" data-comments="392" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48711981" data-time="1782682959">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://theintercept.com/2026/06/26/daniel-sanchez-estrada-zines-prairieland-free-speech/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>30-year sentence for transporting zines is a five-alarm fire for free speech</span><span class='zh'>运输 zines 获30年监禁：对言论自由的严重警报</span></a><span class="dom">theintercept.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The case of a man sentenced to 30 years for transporting zines highlights how anti-distribution laws can chill free speech and independent media.</span><span class='zh'>一名男子因运输 zines 被判处30年监禁，凸显打击分发的法律如何对言论自由和独立媒体造成威慑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The case of a man sentenced to 30 years for transporting zines highlights how anti-distribution laws can chill free speech and independent media. The severity underscores concerns about criminal penalties for sharing alternative viewpoints, spurring debates on reform and civil liberties.</span><span class='zh'>一名男子因运输 zines 被判处30年监禁，凸显打击分发的法律如何对言论自由和独立媒体造成威慑。如此严厉的刑罚引发对相关法律改革与公民自由的广泛关注与讨论。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://europeancorrespondent.com/en/r/the-us-ambassador-had-belgian-police-stop-our-reporting" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://images.europeancorrespondent.com/attRp54hjyayl13fH/public' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='europeancorrespondent.com' data-c='#f97316' data-tag='Politics'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">21</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://europeancorrespondent.com/en/r/the-us-ambassador-had-belgian-police-stop-our-reporting" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The US ambassador had Belgian police stop our reporting</span><span class='zh'>美大使让比利时警方叫停报道</span></a><span class="dom">europeancorrespondent.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A US ambassador allegedly prompted Belgian police to halt our reporting, stoking debate about diplomatic influence on journalism in Europe.</span><span class='zh'>据称美国大使促使比利时警方叫停报道，引发欧洲范围内对外交干预新闻自由的讨论。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A US ambassador allegedly prompted Belgian police to halt our reporting, stoking debate about diplomatic influence on journalism in Europe. The episode highlights the risks to press freedom when state actors attempt to control coverage abroad and may invite scrutiny of diplomatic immunity and journalistic independence.</span><span class='zh'>据称美国大使促使比利时警方叫停报道，引发欧洲范围内对外交干预新闻自由的讨论。此事件凸显当国家行为体试图干预境外报道时，媒体自由可能受到的寒蝉效应，以及对外交豁免与言论自由监管的关注。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>679</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48730608' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>319</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48724230" data-id="48724230" data-tags="Web|Privacy|Security" data-score="667" data-comments="378" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48724230" data-time="1782762568">
<a class="thumb" href="https://hccf.onmy.cloud/2026/06/21/reclaiming-our-digital-selves-hccfs-vision-for-a-human-centered-top-level-domain/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#06b6d4' data-tag='Web'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=hccf.onmy.cloud&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>hccf.onmy.cloud</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">22</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hccf.onmy.cloud/2026/06/21/reclaiming-our-digital-selves-hccfs-vision-for-a-human-centered-top-level-domain/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>.self: A new top-level domain designed to support self-hosting</span><span class='zh'>.self：为自托管设计的新顶级域名</span></a><span class="dom">hccf.onmy.cloud</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>.self is proposed as a human-centered top-level domain intended to empower individuals to run personal, self-hosted services with less reliance on centralized providers.</span><span class='zh'>“.self” 被提出作为以人为本的顶级域名，旨在让个人能够在较少依赖中心化服务商的情况下运营自托管服务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>.self is proposed as a human-centered top-level domain intended to empower individuals to run personal, self-hosted services with less reliance on centralized providers. The idea signals a shift in DNS governance toward user-owned namespaces and could spur tooling and registries to support personal data sovereignty, though practical adoption and security models remain to be worked out.</span><span class='zh'>“.self” 被提出作为以人为本的顶级域名，旨在让个人能够在较少依赖中心化服务商的情况下运营自托管服务。这一构想推动域名系统向用户自有命名空间的方向发展，可能促成支持个人数据主权的工具与注册体系，但实际落地的采纳度与安全模型尚待完善。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>667</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48724230' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>378</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48797916" data-id="48797916" data-tags="Open Source|Hardware" data-score="651" data-comments="159" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48797916" data-time="1783285386">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.opentools.studio/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.opentools.studio/web/image/1096-6c03f418/Open-printer_2_black-prints.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='opentools.studio' data-c='#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">23</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.opentools.studio/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenPrinter</span><span class='zh'>OpenPrinter：开放打印机项目</span></a><span class="dom">opentools.studio</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenPrinter describes an open-source printing project with modular firmware and hardware design, aiming to reduce vendor lock-in and empower communities to customize printers.</span><span class='zh'>OpenPrinter 是一个开放打印机项目，强调可模块化固件与硬件设计，旨在降低厂商锁定并让社区参与定制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenPrinter describes an open-source printing project with modular firmware and hardware design, aiming to reduce vendor lock-in and empower communities to customize printers. The initiative could accelerate repairability, transparency, and ecosystem innovation for makers and businesses alike.</span><span class='zh'>OpenPrinter 是一个开放打印机项目，强调可模块化固件与硬件设计，旨在降低厂商锁定并让社区参与定制。该方向可能促进可修复性、透明度和生态创新，但也需关注可持续性与供应链问题。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>8.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>651</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48797916' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>159</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48786781" data-id="48786781" data-tags="Privacy|Security" data-score="646" data-comments="368" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786781" data-time="1783183510">
<a class="thumb" href="https://javoriuski.com/post/youtube" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://javoriuski.com/assets/youtube.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='javoriuski.com' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">24</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://javoriuski.com/post/youtube" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Leaking YouTube creators&#x27; private videos</span><span class='zh'>泄露 YouTube 创作者的私人视频</span></a><span class="dom">javoriuski.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Private videos of YouTube creators have surfaced, underscoring how gaps in privacy controls and access management can leak sensitive content.</span><span class='zh'>有些 YouTube 创作者的私人视频被泄露，暴露出隐私控制与访问管理的薄弱环节。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Private videos of YouTube creators have surfaced, underscoring how gaps in privacy controls and access management can leak sensitive content. The incident spotlights the friction between creator workflows, platform safeguards, and third-party storage or collaboration tools. It reinforces the need for strict access auditing, multi-factor authentication, and encrypted pipelines in large creator ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>有些 YouTube 创作者的私人视频被泄露，暴露出隐私控制与访问管理的薄弱环节。事件凸显了创作者工作流、平台防护与第三方存储/协作工具之间的矛盾。需要加强访问审计、启用多因素认证，以及在大型创作者生态系统中实施端到端加密和更严格的数据管控。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://weblog.rogueamoeba.com/2026/06/26/free-the-icons/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Free the Icons</span><span class='zh'>解放图标资源</span></a><span class="dom">weblog.rogueamoeba.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rogue Amoeba argues for freeing the icons used in software from restrictive licenses, advocating permissive, open licensing to accelerate UI design and interoperability.</span><span class='zh'>Rogue Amoeba 提出释放图标资源的主张，主张对图标采用更开放、宽松的许可，以加速用户界面设计与跨项目协作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rogue Amoeba argues for freeing the icons used in software from restrictive licenses, advocating permissive, open licensing to accelerate UI design and interoperability.</span><span class='zh'>Rogue Amoeba 提出释放图标资源的主张，主张对图标采用更开放、宽松的许可，以加速用户界面设计与跨项目协作。此举可能降低开发者的授权门槛，促进自由/开源项目的可用性，但具体的许可方案与落地路径仍待讨论。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Mullvad VPN&#x27;s CEO is reported as the main financier of the far-right Örebro party in Sweden, raising questions about platform neutrality and trust for privacy-focused services.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Mullvad VPN 的 CEO 是瑞典极右翼 Örebro 党的主要资助人，对注重隐私的服务的中立性和信任度带来质疑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Mullvad VPN&#x27;s CEO is reported as the main financier of the far-right Örebro party in Sweden, raising questions about platform neutrality and trust for privacy-focused services. The revelation prompts discussion about funding sources and corporate ethics in the privacy space.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，Mullvad VPN 的 CEO 是瑞典极右翼 Örebro 党的主要资助人，对注重隐私的服务的中立性和信任度带来质疑。此事引发对资金来源与科技隐私领域企业伦理的关注。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Podman 6.0.0 marks a major milestone with expanded rootless container support, improved OCI compatibility, and streamlined tooling.</span><span class='zh'>6.0.0 版本标志着 Podman 的一个重要里程碑，增强了无根容器支持、改进的 OCI 兼容性以及更简化的工具链。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://reclaimthenet.org/sony-deletes-551-studiocanal-movies-playstation-owners-paid-for" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sony Deletes 551 Movies PlayStation Owners Paid For</span><span class='zh'>索尼删除了 PlayStation 用户购买的 551 部电影</span></a><span class="dom">reclaimthenet.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sony reportedly removed 551 StudioCanal titles from PlayStation libraries, affecting owners who thought they had purchased those films.</span><span class='zh'>据报导，索尼从 PlayStation 库中移除了 551 部 StudioCanal 的电影，影响了以为自己购买过的用户。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sony reportedly removed 551 StudioCanal titles from PlayStation libraries, affecting owners who thought they had purchased those films. The incident underlines how digital ownership hinges on licensing and can disappear with rights changes. It raises questions about the permanence of digital libraries and consumer protections.</span><span class='zh'>据报导，索尼从 PlayStation 库中移除了 551 部 StudioCanal 的电影，影响了以为自己购买过的用户。事件凸显数字所有权依赖于许可，权利变更就可能导致内容消失。引发对数字馆藏永久性与消费者保护的思考。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://opensourcelowtech.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Open Source Low Tech</span><span class='zh'>开源低技术</span></a><span class="dom">opensourcelowtech.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Open Source Low Tech advocates practical, accessible open-source approaches to everyday tech.</span><span class='zh'>Open Source Low Tech 提倡以实用、易获得的开源方法来构建日常技术。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Open Source Low Tech advocates practical, accessible open-source approaches to everyday tech. It emphasizes simplicity, transparency, and reusability as core values, not just flashy features.</span><span class='zh'>Open Source Low Tech 提倡以实用、易获得的开源方法来构建日常技术。它强调简单性、透明度和可重复使用性作为核心价值，而不仅仅是花哨的功能。文章还介绍了为何低门槛、开源的选择能够为开发者和创客带来更易维护的系统。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/jun/29/supreme-court-geofence-warrants-case-decision" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>US Supreme Court rules geofence warrants require constitutional protections</span><span class='zh'>美国最高法院裁定地理围栏检 warrants 需宪法保护</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Supreme Court ruled geofence warrants must be protected by Fourth Amendment standards, reinforcing privacy safeguards for location data.</span><span class='zh'>最高法院裁定地理围栏搜查须适用第四修正案标准，加强对定位数据的隐私保护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Supreme Court ruled geofence warrants must be protected by Fourth Amendment standards, reinforcing privacy safeguards for location data. The decision shapes how law enforcement can access location information and how tech platforms respond to such requests.</span><span class='zh'>最高法院裁定地理围栏搜查须适用第四修正案标准，加强对定位数据的隐私保护。这一判决影响执法获取位置信息的边界以及科技平台对相关请求的应对。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.gamingonlinux.com/2026/07/valve-open-source-the-steam-machine-e-ink-screen-so-you-can-make-your-own/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Valve open-source the Steam Machine e-ink screen so you can make your own</span><span class='zh'>Valve 将 Steam Machine 的电子墨水屏开源，教你自制</span></a><span class="dom">gamingonlinux.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Valve releases the e-ink display design from Steam Machine as open source, inviting creators to reuse and remix for their own projects.</span><span class='zh'>Valve 将 Steam Machine 的电子墨水屏设计开源，鼓励创作者在自定义项目中复用与改造。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Valve releases the e-ink display design from Steam Machine as open source, inviting creators to reuse and remix for their own projects. This openness lowers barriers for hardware experimentation and highlights a community driven approach to hardware prototyping.</span><span class='zh'>Valve 将 Steam Machine 的电子墨水屏设计开源，鼓励创作者在自定义项目中复用与改造。这一开源举措降低了硬件试验门槛，凸显硬件原型设计的社区驱动性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tedium.co/2026/07/01/online-web-forums-retrospective/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bring back crappy forums</span><span class='zh'>让糟糕论坛卷土重来</span></a><span class="dom">tedium.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A retrospective argues for reviving the rough-and-tumble forum culture that predates modern platforms—less polish, more candid discussion, and simpler moderation.</span><span class='zh'>该文回顾并主张让糟糕但充满活力的论坛文化回归——强调直率讨论、低门槛与简化的社区治理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A retrospective argues for reviving the rough-and-tumble forum culture that predates modern platforms—less polish, more candid discussion, and simpler moderation. It examines what early forums did well and what modern networks could borrow, from lightweight threading to signal-to-noise management.</span><span class='zh'>该文回顾并主张让糟糕但充满活力的论坛文化回归——强调直率讨论、低门槛与简化的社区治理。它讨论早期论坛的优点，以及现代社群应借鉴的经验，如简化的主题讨论与信号噪声管理。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.caseyliss.com/2026/7/2/carplay-is-additive-you-dolts" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>CarPlay Is Additive</span><span class='zh'>CarPlay 是增补而非替代</span></a><span class="dom">caseyliss.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author contends that CarPlay augments existing car interfaces rather than replacing them, adding convenience without solving deeper UX and safety trade-offs in automotive software.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为 CarPlay 是对车载界面的补充，而非替代，带来便利但并未解决汽车软件中的深层 UX 与安全权衡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author contends that CarPlay augments existing car interfaces rather than replacing them, adding convenience without solving deeper UX and safety trade-offs in automotive software.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为 CarPlay 是对车载界面的补充，而非替代，带来便利但并未解决汽车软件中的深层 UX 与安全权衡。文章强调苹果的策略改变了用户预期和生态格局，迫使厂商在整合与保留车辆专属控制间寻找平衡。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Science is a new product focused on scientific workloads, offering specialized reasoning and data-handling features for researchers.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Science 是面向科学工作负载的新产品，提供专门的推理和数据处理能力，帮助研究人员加速分析与建模。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Science is a new product focused on scientific workloads, offering specialized reasoning and data-handling features for researchers. It aims to speed up literature review, modeling, and experimentation workflows for scientists.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Science 是面向科学工作负载的新产品，提供专门的推理和数据处理能力，帮助研究人员加速分析与建模。面向研究人员和数据科学家，旨在优化文献综述、实验设计与数据分析的工作流。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://antoine.fi/mri-analysis-using-claude-code-opus" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I used Claude Code to get a second opinion on my MRI</span><span class='zh'>我用 Claude Code 为我的 MRI 做了第二意见</span></a><span class="dom">antoine.fi</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A clinician uses Claude Code to review an MRI, illustrating how AI copilots can augment radiology while raising caveats.</span><span class='zh'>作者用 Claude Code 对 MRI 进行了二次意见的评估，展示了 AI 辅助工具在放射学领域的潜在作用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A clinician uses Claude Code to review an MRI, illustrating how AI copilots can augment radiology while raising caveats. This kind of usage emphasizes AI as an assistive, not replacement, tool in clinical workflows.</span><span class='zh'>作者用 Claude Code 对 MRI 进行了二次意见的评估，展示了 AI 辅助工具在放射学领域的潜在作用。文中指出了如快速获取第二意见和广泛模式识别等实际好处，同时警示了可靠性、幻觉输出与隐私方面的局限性，强调 AI 仅应作为临床流程中的辅助工具。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores how Costco’s membership model, bulk-buying, limited SKUs, and disciplined logistics create a different kind of customer loyalty and price discipline than Amazon’s marketplace approach.</span><span class='zh'>文章分析 Costco 的会员制、批量采购、有限 SKU 和严密的物流管理，展示其与亚马逊的不同商业模式与竞争优势，称其为现代零售中的‘反亚马逊’。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores how Costco’s membership model, bulk-buying, limited SKUs, and disciplined logistics create a different kind of customer loyalty and price discipline than Amazon’s marketplace approach. The piece argues these choices position Costco as the anti-Amazon in the modern retail era.</span><span class='zh'>文章分析 Costco 的会员制、批量采购、有限 SKU 和严密的物流管理，展示其与亚马逊的不同商业模式与竞争优势，称其为现代零售中的‘反亚马逊’。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://asahilinux.org/2026/06/progress-report-7-1/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Asahi Linux 7.1 Progress Report</span><span class='zh'>Asahi Linux 7.1 进展报告</span></a><span class="dom">asahilinux.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Asahi Linux 7.1 delivers new kernel improvements, broader Apple Silicon support, and performance tweaks aimed at making Macs run Linux more smoothly.</span><span class='zh'>Asahi Linux 7.1 的进展包括内核改进、对 Apple Silicon 的更广泛支持以及性能优化，旨在让在 Mac 上运行 Linux 更加顺畅。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Asahi Linux 7.1 delivers new kernel improvements, broader Apple Silicon support, and performance tweaks aimed at making Macs run Linux more smoothly. The update narrows hardware gaps and enhances stability for everyday use and development on Apple hardware. It signals ongoing momentum for Linux on consumer devices.</span><span class='zh'>Asahi Linux 7.1 的进展包括内核改进、对 Apple Silicon 的更广泛支持以及性能优化，旨在让在 Mac 上运行 Linux 更加顺畅。此次更新缩小了硬件兼容性差距、提升了日常使用与开发的稳定性，继续推动 Linux 在消费级设备上的应用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.pcgamer.com/gaming-industry/open-source-game-engine-godot-will-no-longer-accept-ai-authored-code-contributions-we-cant-trust-heavy-users-of-ai-to-understand-their-code-enough-to-fix-it/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Godot will no longer accept AI-authored code contributions</span><span class='zh'>Godot 将不再接受 AI 编写的代码贡献</span></a><span class="dom">pcgamer.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Godot Foundation bans AI-authored code contributions to the core, citing concerns about reliability and maintainability.</span><span class='zh'>Godot 基金会宣布不再接受 AI 编写的代码提交，理由是对可靠性和可维护性的担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Godot Foundation bans AI-authored code contributions to the core, citing concerns about reliability and maintainability. The policy underscores the ongoing tension in OSS between AI-assisted tooling and the need for human oversight in critical code.</span><span class='zh'>Godot 基金会宣布不再接受 AI 编写的代码提交，理由是对可靠性和可维护性的担忧。此举凸显开源社区在 AI 辅助编码和人类审阅之间的持续张力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mynintendonews.com/2026/06/26/nintendo-has-raised-its-employees-base-salary-by-10/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nintendo has raised its employees base salary by 10%</span><span class='zh'>任天堂将员工基本薪资提高10%</span></a><span class="dom">mynintendonews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nintendo announced a 10% raise to base salaries, signaling wage growth in a major entertainment tech company.</span><span class='zh'>任天堂宣布将员工基本工资提高10%，显示出在劳动力紧张的市场中公司对人才的重视。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nintendo announced a 10% raise to base salaries, signaling wage growth in a major entertainment tech company. The move may aid retention and morale amid tight labor markets while squeezing margins modestly. It also mirrors broader wage-tightening and inflation-adaptation trends in the industry.</span><span class='zh'>任天堂宣布将员工基本工资提高10%，显示出在劳动力紧张的市场中公司对人才的重视。此举有助于提升留任与士气，但可能对利润率产生一定挤压，反映出行业的薪资调整与通胀应对趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://stefan.schueller.net/posts/the-free-market-lie/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why Switzerland has 25 gbit internet and America doesn&#x27;t</span><span class='zh'>自由市场的谬误：为何瑞士有25 Gbit互联网而美国没有</span></a><span class="dom">stefan.schueller.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that Switzerland’s fast, ubiquitous fiber is the result of deliberate public policy, infrastructure sharing, and local competition rather than a laissez-faire telecom market.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，瑞士的高速光纤来自有意的公共政策、基础设施共享和地方性竞争，而不是放任的自由市场。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that Switzerland’s fast, ubiquitous fiber is the result of deliberate public policy, infrastructure sharing, and local competition rather than a laissez-faire telecom market. It contrasts that with structural barriers in the U.S.—incumbent incentives, pole and rights-of-way issues, and regulatory choices—that have kept speeds and deployment lagging despite market rhetoric.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，瑞士的高速光纤来自有意的公共政策、基础设施共享和地方性竞争，而不是放任的自由市场。与之相对，美国因既得利益、线路与通行权问题及监管选择，导致部署和速率长期落后。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>534</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48770647' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>429</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48763035" data-id="48763035" data-tags="Security" data-score="531" data-comments="222" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48763035" data-time="1783005916">
<a class="thumb" href="https://mathstodon.xyz/@iblech/116769502749142438" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=mathstodon.xyz&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>mathstodon.xyz</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">45</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mathstodon.xyz/@iblech/116769502749142438" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Since Linux 6.9, LUKS suspend stopped wiping disk-encryption keys from memory</span><span class='zh'>自 Linux 6.9 起，LUKS 休眠不再从内存擦除磁盘加密密钥</span></a><span class="dom">mathstodon.xyz</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Linux 6.9 introduced a change where LUKS suspend no longer clears in-memory keys, trading security for faster resume.</span><span class='zh'>自 Linux 6.9 起，LUKS 休眠不再从内存中清除磁盘加密密钥，提升恢复速度的同时扩大了内存中密钥暴露的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Linux 6.9 introduced a change where LUKS suspend no longer clears in-memory keys, trading security for faster resume. The decision highlights the ongoing tension between safeguarding encryption keys and preserving usability in sleep states.</span><span class='zh'>自 Linux 6.9 起，LUKS 休眠不再从内存中清除磁盘加密密钥，提升恢复速度的同时扩大了内存中密钥暴露的风险。这一取舍凸显了在安全性与可用性之间的持续权衡。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.5</span><span class='m ic-up'>531</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48763035' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>222</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48768951" data-id="48768951" data-tags="Privacy|Legal|AI|Hardware" data-score="524" data-comments="185" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48768951" data-time="1783036443">
<a class="thumb" href="https://righttointelligence.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://righttointelligence.org/og.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='righttointelligence.org' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">46</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://righttointelligence.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Protect your right to run local AI</span><span class='zh'>保护本地运行 AI 的权利</span></a><span class="dom">righttointelligence.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Campaigns argue users should be allowed to run AI models locally, offline, on their own hardware without cloud dependence.</span><span class='zh'>倡导者认为人们应能在本地硬件上离线运行 AI 模型，避免对云端的依赖。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Campaigns argue users should be allowed to run AI models locally, offline, on their own hardware without cloud dependence. That shift would boost privacy, data control, and resilience, though hardware costs and licensing remain barriers. The piece frames why this matters to developers and enthusiasts who want autonomy over their AI tools.</span><span class='zh'>倡导者认为人们应能在本地硬件上离线运行 AI 模型，避免对云端的依赖。这一转变能提升隐私、数据掌控和系统韧性，但硬件成本和许可证限制仍是现实障碍。文章聚焦对于希望掌控 AI 工具的开发者和爱好者为何重要。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>524</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48768951' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>185</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48761118" data-id="48761118" data-tags="Career" data-score="516" data-comments="79" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48761118" data-time="1782998362">
<a class="thumb" href="https://pradyuprasad.com/writings/how-to-ask-for-help/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://pradyuprasad.com/card.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='pradyuprasad.com' data-c='#2dd4bf' data-tag='Career'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">47</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pradyuprasad.com/writings/how-to-ask-for-help/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How to ask for help from people who don&#x27;t know you</span><span class='zh'>如何向不认识你的人寻求帮助</span></a><span class="dom">pradyuprasad.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ask for help from people who do not know you can work if you build credibility quickly, keep requests concise, and offer value or an easy path to say yes.</span><span class='zh'>向陌生人求助往往需要先建立可信度、给出明确的请求并提供回报价值。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ask for help from people who do not know you can work if you build credibility quickly, keep requests concise, and offer value or an easy path to say yes. The guidance emphasizes clear, respectful asks and the strategic use of warm introductions to overcome friction.</span><span class='zh'>向陌生人求助往往需要先建立可信度、给出明确的请求并提供回报价值。文章给出一系列实用策略：简短有力的表述、附带背景信息、以及便于对方快速答复的具体问题。把对方从“陌生人”变成愿意帮助的对象，核心在于高效沟通和善用引介。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/06/kids-act-would-require-age-checks-get-online" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The KIDS Act would require age checks to get online</span><span class='zh'>KIDS Act 将强制在线年龄验证</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The KIDS Act proposes mandatory online age verification, aiming to shield minors but raising privacy and profiling concerns.</span><span class='zh'>KIDS Act 提议对在线服务强制进行年龄验证，旨在保护未成年人；但也引发隐私与画像化担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The KIDS Act proposes mandatory online age verification, aiming to shield minors but raising privacy and profiling concerns. Critics warn it could normalize surveillance and create security risks for users.</span><span class='zh'>KIDS Act 提议对在线服务强制进行年龄验证，旨在保护未成年人；但也引发隐私与画像化担忧。批评者警告这可能使监控常态化并带来安全风险。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>504</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48706560' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>403</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48786838" data-id="48786838" data-tags="Data|Legal|AI" data-score="503" data-comments="290" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786838" data-time="1783183886">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://software.annas-archive.gl/AnnaArchivist/annas-archive/-/work_items/234" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google Books (or similar) all book scans – $200k bounty (2025)</span><span class='zh'>谷歌图书（或同类）对完整书籍扫描的20万美元悬赏（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">software.annas-archive.gl</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A $200k bounty aims to incentivize scanning of an entire book corpus, similar to Google Books.</span><span class='zh'>一项价值20万美元的悬赏旨在推动对整套书籍进行数字化扫描，这类似于谷歌图书的计划。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A $200k bounty aims to incentivize scanning of an entire book corpus, similar to Google Books. This initiative could accelerate digitization, OCR accuracy, and AI training data availability, while raising licensing, copyright, and provenance questions. Expect debates over public-domain scope, fair use, and the balance between accessibility and rights.</span><span class='zh'>一项价值20万美元的悬赏旨在推动对整套书籍进行数字化扫描，这类似于谷歌图书的计划。此举可能加速书籍的数字化、光学字符识别的准确性，以及可供机器学习训练的数据集，但也引发版权、授权与来源等问题。公众领域、使用权与可获取性的平衡将成为讨论焦点。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>503</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48786838' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>290</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48753715" data-id="48753715" data-tags="Programming" data-score="503" data-comments="350" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48753715" data-time="1782943416">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://zcode.z.ai/en" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ZCode – Harness for GLM-5.2</span><span class='zh'>ZCode：GLM-5.2 的测试套件</span></a><span class="dom">zcode.z.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ZCode releases a harness for GLM-5.2, offering an end-to-end toolkit to run prompts, collect metrics, and compare outputs.</span><span class='zh'>ZCode 推出 GLM-5.2 的测试套件，提供从输入到结果评估的一体化工具，便于执行提示、收集指标和对比输出。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ZCode releases a harness for GLM-5.2, offering an end-to-end toolkit to run prompts, collect metrics, and compare outputs. This reduces the friction of experimentation and makes benchmarking more reproducible for ML researchers and developers.</span><span class='zh'>ZCode 推出 GLM-5.2 的测试套件，提供从输入到结果评估的一体化工具，便于执行提示、收集指标和对比输出。降低研究者的试验门槛，便于可重复的基准比较。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://box2d.org/posts/2026/06/announcing-box3d/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Box3D, an open source 3D physics engine</span><span class='zh'>宣布发布 Box3D：开源的 3D 物理引擎</span></a><span class="dom">box2d.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Box3D arrives as an open-source successor in 3D physics engines, following Box2D lineage.</span><span class='zh'>Box3D 作为 3D 物理引擎的开源继任者加入，沿袭 Box2D 的传统，目标是提供健壮的刚体动力学、模块化设计以及更易的集成，鼓励社区贡献。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Box3D arrives as an open-source successor in 3D physics engines, following Box2D lineage. It aims to deliver robust rigid-body dynamics with a modular design and easier integration for games and simulations, inviting community contributions. If you build 3D physics into your project, Box3D could become a go-to option without licensing friction.</span><span class='zh'>Box3D 作为 3D 物理引擎的开源继任者加入，沿袭 Box2D 的传统，目标是提供健壮的刚体动力学、模块化设计以及更易的集成，鼓励社区贡献。若你的项目需要 3D 物理，这个引擎可能成为无许可负担的首选选项。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://libertystreeteconomics.newyorkfed.org/2026/06/the-post-covid-decline-in-the-labor-share/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://libertystreeteconomics.newyorkfed.org/wp-content/uploads/sites/2/2026/06/LSE_2026_labor-share_schuh_460.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='libertystreeteconomics.newyorkfed.org' data-c='#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">52</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://libertystreeteconomics.newyorkfed.org/2026/06/the-post-covid-decline-in-the-labor-share/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The labor share of income in the US is at its lowest post-war level</span><span class='zh'>美国劳动收入份额降至战后最低水平</span></a><span class="dom">libertystreeteconomics.newyorkfed.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>US labor’s share of income has declined to a postwar low, signaling that productivity gains and capital income are capturing a growing slice of economic output.</span><span class='zh'>美国劳动者在国民总收入中的份额降至战后最低水平，反映生产率增益和资本收入正在分得更大份额。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>US labor’s share of income has declined to a postwar low, signaling that productivity gains and capital income are capturing a growing slice of economic output. The trend implies slower wage growth for workers and potential pressure on consumer demand, with implications for inequality and policy debates. Analysts see a shift in income distribution driven by automation, globalization, and pricing power.</span><span class='zh'>美国劳动者在国民总收入中的份额降至战后最低水平，反映生产率增益和资本收入正在分得更大份额。此趋势意味着劳动者工资增长可能放缓，消费力受压，且可能加剧收入不平等。分析师将关注结构性因素如何影响工资与投资回报的长期分配。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://unsung.aresluna.org/if-youre-a-button-you-have-one-job/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If you&#x27;re a button, you have one job</span><span class='zh'>按钮只有一个职责</span></a><span class="dom">unsung.aresluna.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A provocative take on the role of a button: it has one job—to trigger actions.</span><span class='zh'>文章强调按钮的本质是执行操作，应正确使用按钮元素以提升可访问性与可预测性，避免将用于导航的元素错用成按钮。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A provocative take on the role of a button: it has one job—to trigger actions. The piece argues for proper semantics and accessibility, urging developers to reserve button elements for actions and use links where appropriate.</span><span class='zh'>文章强调按钮的本质是执行操作，应正确使用按钮元素以提升可访问性与可预测性，避免将用于导航的元素错用成按钮。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/librepods-org/librepods" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Librepods: AirPods liberated</span><span class='zh'>Librepods：AirPods 自由化项目</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Librepods aims to liberate AirPods by removing vendor-imposed constraints and enabling user-controlled firmware experiments.</span><span class='zh'>Librepods 通过移除厂商设定的限制、让用户能够控制固件来实现 AirPods 的自由化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Librepods aims to liberate AirPods by removing vendor-imposed constraints and enabling user-controlled firmware experiments. The project advocates for ownership and privacy but may raise legal, warranty, and security questions.</span><span class='zh'>Librepods 通过移除厂商设定的限制、让用户能够控制固件来实现 AirPods 的自由化。该项目倡导拥有权与隐私，但也可能带来法律、保修与安全方面的问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.thebignewsletter.com/p/crime-pays-the-egg-bandits-made-a" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Egg Bandits Made a Thousand Times the Fine They Just Paid for Price Fixing</span><span class='zh'>蛋价共谋者缴纳的罚款远不及其收益</span></a><span class="dom">thebignewsletter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Fines for egg-price fixers are typically dwarfed by the profits they earned from the scheme.</span><span class='zh'>蛋价垄断案中，罚款往往只是短期成本，远低于参与者从价格操纵中获得的长期收益。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Fines for egg-price fixers are typically dwarfed by the profits they earned from the scheme. This piece argues for tougher penalties, and possibly criminal charges, to deter cartels and protect consumers.</span><span class='zh'>蛋价垄断案中，罚款往往只是短期成本，远低于参与者从价格操纵中获得的长期收益。文章强调只有提高处罚力度、甚至引入刑事责任，才能真正抑制此类共谋。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A comprehensive guide decodes every field in htop and top on Linux, explaining what each metric means and how to interpret them in practice.</span><span class='zh'>这篇详尽指南逐一解释 Linux 上 htop/top 的每个字段和指标，以及如何据此解读性能信号、定位瓶颈。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A comprehensive guide decodes every field in htop and top on Linux, explaining what each metric means and how to interpret them in practice. It helps practitioners diagnose performance bottlenecks beyond surface readings. Includes tips on CPU/memory usage, process states, and how sampling affects observations.</span><span class='zh'>这篇详尽指南逐一解释 Linux 上 htop/top 的每个字段和指标，以及如何据此解读性能信号、定位瓶颈。提供实用建议，帮助读者理解 CPU/内存、进程状态和 I/O 等数据在监控中的含义及刷新对观察的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://investors.rocketlabcorp.com/news-releases/news-release-details/rocket-lab-acquire-iridium-historic-deal-creating-fully" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rocketlab acquires Iridium</span><span class='zh'>Rocket Lab 收购 Iridium：历史性交易</span></a><span class="dom">investors.rocketlabcorp.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rocket Lab&#x27;s planned acquisition of Iridium Communications would create a vertically integrated space services company, combining launch, satellite operations, and communications.</span><span class='zh'>Rocket Lab 将收购 Iridium，整合发射、卫星运营与通信，可能打造垂直整合的太空服务公司。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rocket Lab&#x27;s planned acquisition of Iridium Communications would create a vertically integrated space services company, combining launch, satellite operations, and communications. The deal signals ongoing consolidation in the space economy and could reshape small-satellite market dynamics. If approved, it may accelerate end-to-end offerings for customers building constellations.</span><span class='zh'>Rocket Lab 将收购 Iridium，整合发射、卫星运营与通信，可能打造垂直整合的太空服务公司。此举反映了太空产业的持续整合，并可能重塑小卫星市场格局。若获批，或将推动面向星座构建的端到端解决方案加速落地。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://makerspet.com/blog/building-an-open-source-robot-vacuum-meet-oomwoo/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Oomwoo, an open-source robot vacuum you build yourself</span><span class='zh'>Oomwoo：一个你自己组装的开源机器人吸尘器</span></a><span class="dom">makerspet.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Oomwoo is an open-source, build-it-yourself robot vacuum kit that lets hobbyists swap hardware and customize software.</span><span class='zh'>Oomwoo 是一个可自行组装的开源机器人吸尘器套件，允许用户定制硬件与软件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Oomwoo is an open-source, build-it-yourself robot vacuum kit that lets hobbyists swap hardware and customize software. It showcases the growing maker movement around home robots, providing transparent designs, firmware, and code. Community-driven projects like this push for accessible robotics and faster iteration.</span><span class='zh'>Oomwoo 是一个可自行组装的开源机器人吸尘器套件，允许用户定制硬件与软件。它体现了家用机器人开源潮流，通过透明的硬件、固件与代码促进社区协作与快速迭代。这样的项目降低入门门槛，推动更多爱好者参与硬件创新。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>FFmpeg 9.1 introduces a new AAC encoder with improved encoding quality and efficiency, offering better stereo handling and options for low-latency workflows.</span><span class='zh'>FFmpeg 9.1 带来全新的 AAC 编码器，提升了编码质量和效率，并在立体声处理与低延迟工作流方面提供改进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>FFmpeg 9.1 introduces a new AAC encoder with improved encoding quality and efficiency, offering better stereo handling and options for low-latency workflows. The update may influence streaming pipelines and compliance considerations for AAC encoding in media workflows.</span><span class='zh'>FFmpeg 9.1 带来全新的 AAC 编码器，提升了编码质量和效率，并在立体声处理与低延迟工作流方面提供改进。此更新对媒体工作流中的 AAC 编码兼容性与流式传输管线可能产生影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A county housing 37 data centers is asking schools to conserve electricity amid rising power costs. It also underscores broader questions about energy pricing, resilience, and the cost of data-driven services.</span><span class='zh'>拥有37座数据中心的县在能源成本上升之际呼吁学校节电。这一举措凸显大型数字基础设施对公共能源预算和电网负担的影响，促使学区优化用电与时段安排，也暴露出能源定价与系统韧性的更广泛问题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.demofox.org/2026/07/01/what-to-learn-to-be-a-graphics-programmer/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What to learn to be a graphics programmer</span><span class='zh'>成为图形程序员要学什么</span></a><span class="dom">blog.demofox.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A practical roadmap that covers the fundamentals: math (linear algebra, vectors, matrices), GPU pipelines, shading languages, and APIs (OpenGL/Vulkan/DirectX).</span><span class='zh'>这篇实用路线图列出成为图形程序员的核心要点，包括线性代数等数学基础、GPU 管线、着色语言以及 OpenGL/Vulkan/DirectX 等 API。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A practical roadmap that covers the fundamentals: math (linear algebra, vectors, matrices), GPU pipelines, shading languages, and APIs (OpenGL/Vulkan/DirectX). It emphasizes building real projects (ray tracers, small engines) and a portfolio to demonstrate skill progression, plus tips on debugging shader code.</span><span class='zh'>这篇实用路线图列出成为图形程序员的核心要点，包括线性代数等数学基础、GPU 管线、着色语言以及 OpenGL/Vulkan/DirectX 等 API。作者建议以小型引擎或光线追踪实现等实战项目来积累作品集，附带调试着色器的实战要点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://torrentfreak.com/european-isps-want-rightsholders-held-accountable-for-overblocking-damage/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>European ISPs Want Rightsholders Held Accountable for Overblocking Damage</span><span class='zh'>欧洲 ISP 希望权利方对过度拦截造成的损害负责</span></a><span class="dom">torrentfreak.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>European ISPs are pushing back on overblocking by rights holders and seeking accountability for damages, signaling ongoing tension between enforcement and civil liberties.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲的运营商正就权利方过度拦截造成的损害追究责任，凸显执法与公民自由之间的持续冲突。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>European ISPs are pushing back on overblocking by rights holders and seeking accountability for damages, signaling ongoing tension between enforcement and civil liberties. The push could influence EU policy on safe harbors, liability, and the economics of content blocking.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲的运营商正就权利方过度拦截造成的损害追究责任，凸显执法与公民自由之间的持续冲突。这一举动可能影响欧盟关于安全港、法律责任及内容拦截经济性的相关政策。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://en.sedaily.com/international/2026/06/29/samsung-sk-hynix-micron-sued-in-us-over-memory-price-fixing" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Samsung, SK Hynix, Micron Sued in US over Memory Price Fixing</span><span class='zh'>三星、SK 海力士、美光在美国因内存价格垄断被起诉</span></a><span class="dom">en.sedaily.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A US lawsuit accuses Samsung, SK Hynix, and Micron of memory price fixing, potentially affecting pricing dynamics and competition in the memory market.</span><span class='zh'>美国对三星、SK海力士和美光提起的价格垄断诉讼指控其串通操纵内存价格，可能改变市场定价与竞争格局，并增加对芯片厂商的监管压力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A US lawsuit accuses Samsung, SK Hynix, and Micron of memory price fixing, potentially affecting pricing dynamics and competition in the memory market. The case adds to antitrust scrutiny of chipmakers and could influence regulatory actions.</span><span class='zh'>美国对三星、SK海力士和美光提起的价格垄断诉讼指控其串通操纵内存价格，可能改变市场定价与竞争格局，并增加对芯片厂商的监管压力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://pudding.cool/2026/06/menu-story/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>5k menus from the New York Public Library’s Buttolph Collection (1880-1920)</span><span class='zh'>纽约公共图书馆 Buttolph 收藏的五千份菜单（1880-1920）</span></a><span class="dom">pudding.cool</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The NYPL’s Buttolph Collection includes around 5,000 menus from 1880–1920, offering a rich dataset for historians and data scientists.</span><span class='zh'>纽约公共图书馆的 Buttolph 收藏约五千份菜单，覆盖 1880–1920 年代，为历史研究者与数据科学家提供丰富的数据集。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The NYPL’s Buttolph Collection includes around 5,000 menus from 1880–1920, offering a rich dataset for historians and data scientists. Text mining these menus reveals shifts in cuisine, pricing, and social norms across a transformative era.</span><span class='zh'>纽约公共图书馆的 Buttolph 收藏约五千份菜单，覆盖 1880–1920 年代，为历史研究者与数据科学家提供丰富的数据集。通过对菜单中的菜名、价格与语言进行挖掘，可以揭示这一变革时期的饮食、定价与社会风俗的变化。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The essay warns that current U.S. laws and enforcement are failing to contain pervasive data collection and surveillance, arguing for urgent legislative and regulatory reforms. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Japan&#x27;s top court rules AI can&#x27;t be listed as inventor on patent applications.</span><span class='zh'>日本最高法院裁定 AI 不能作为发明人出现在专利申请中，意味着发明人必须是人类。</span></p>

That means humans must be named as inventors, keeping a clear line between human ingenuity and AI contributions with implications for IP governance.</span><span class='zh'>日本最高法院裁定 AI 不能作为发明人出现在专利申请中，意味着发明人必须是人类。这对 AI 辅助的发明及未来的知识产权治理有现实影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.engadget.com/2203000/flock-cameras-recording-license-plate/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Flock cameras track more than your license plate, and they&#x27;re spreading fast</span><span class='zh'>Flock 摄像头不仅追踪车牌，扩张速度惊人</span></a><span class="dom">engadget.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New reporting suggests Flock cameras collect more than license plate data, and deployments are rapidly expanding across cities.</span><span class='zh'>Flock 摄像头的监控范围已超越车牌数据，且部署正迅速在各城市扩张。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New reporting suggests Flock cameras collect more than license plate data, and deployments are rapidly expanding across cities. Privacy advocates warn that expanding surveillance features could track individuals beyond their vehicles, prompting regulatory scrutiny. The rapid rollouts could reshape public-space monitoring norms and vendor expectations.</span><span class='zh'>Flock 摄像头的监控范围已超越车牌数据，且部署正迅速在各城市扩张。隐私倡导者警告，日益扩大的监控能力可能超出车辆范围，触发监管审查。快速扩张可能重塑公共空间的监控规范以及厂商预期。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://popcar.bearblog.dev/its-about-ownership/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>It&#x27;s not about physical vs. digital games, it&#x27;s about ownership</span><span class='zh'>不是实体/数字游戏之争，而是关于所有权的问题</span></a><span class="dom">popcar.bearblog.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that ownership—not format—drives value in gaming, covering DRM, portability, and platform control that shape how players access and keep games.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，游戏真正的分水岭在于所有权，而非物理还是数字的形式；包括数字版权管理、跨平台可移植性和平台控制等因素，决定玩家对游戏的长期拥有与访问。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that ownership—not format—drives value in gaming, covering DRM, portability, and platform control that shape how players access and keep games.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，游戏真正的分水岭在于所有权，而非物理还是数字的形式；包括数字版权管理、跨平台可移植性和平台控制等因素，决定玩家对游戏的长期拥有与访问。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/FractalFir/crustc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>crustc: entirety of `rustc`, translated to C</span><span class='zh'>crustc：将整个 rustc 翻译为 C</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>crustc is an ambitious project that translates the entirety of the Rust compiler into C, aiming to make rustc more portable and easier to bootstrap on unusual toolchains.</span><span class='zh'>crustc 是个雄心勃勃的工程，把整个 Rust 编译器翻译成 C，目标是提升 rustc 在非常规工具链上的可移植性和引导能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>crustc is an ambitious project that translates the entirety of the Rust compiler into C, aiming to make rustc more portable and easier to bootstrap on unusual toolchains. The repo is experimental but useful as a study in compiler architecture, portability trade-offs, and alternate bootstrapping strategies.</span><span class='zh'>crustc 是个雄心勃勃的工程，把整个 Rust 编译器翻译成 C，目标是提升 rustc 在非常规工具链上的可移植性和引导能力。该项目处于实验性阶段，但对研究编译器架构、可移植性权衡以及替代引导策略很有价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mathstodon.xyz/@mjd/115096720350507897" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The primary purpose of code review is to find code that will be hard to maintain</span><span class='zh'>代码评审的首要目的是发现难以维护的代码</span></a><span class="dom">mathstodon.xyz</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Code review should primarily reveal maintainability risks rather than only catching bugs.</span><span class='zh'>代码评审的核心目标是揭示那些后续难以维护的设计与实现问题，如高耦合、模糊的接口和不明显的依赖关系。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Code review should primarily reveal maintainability risks rather than only catching bugs. By focusing on readability, modularity, and clear interfaces, teams reduce future debt and speed advancement.</span><span class='zh'>代码评审的核心目标是揭示那些后续难以维护的设计与实现问题，如高耦合、模糊的接口和不明显的依赖关系。通过关注可维护性，团队可减少技术债务并提升长期开发效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/30364" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GPT-5.5 Codex reasoning-token clustering may be leading to degraded performance</span><span class='zh'>GPT-5.5 Codex 推理令牌聚类可能导致性能下降</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers report that clustering reasoning tokens in GPT-5.5 Codex-style workflows may degrade performance, suggesting a misalignment between token organization and inference efficiency.</span><span class='zh'>研究指出在 GPT-5.5/Codex 风格工作流中对推理令牌进行聚类可能降低性能，表明令牌组织方式与推理效率之间存在错配。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers report that clustering reasoning tokens in GPT-5.5 Codex-style workflows may degrade performance, suggesting a misalignment between token organization and inference efficiency. The finding has implications for how developers architect long-chain reasoning or tool-assisted prompts in production systems.</span><span class='zh'>研究指出在 GPT-5.5/Codex 风格工作流中对推理令牌进行聚类可能降低性能，表明令牌组织方式与推理效率之间存在错配。这一发现对在生产系统中设计长链推理或工具辅助提示的架构设计具有重要意义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.thefocalpoints.com/p/new-study-frog-derived-gut-bacterium" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Frog-derived gut bacterium eradicates tumors in mice</span><span class='zh'>来自青蛙肠道的细菌在小鼠体内根除肿瘤</span></a><span class="dom">thefocalpoints.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A gut bacterium derived from frogs has eliminated tumors in mice, suggesting a microbiome-based approach to cancer therapy.</span><span class='zh'>来自青蛙肠道的细菌在小鼠身上实现肿瘤清除，显示了微生物组干预在癌症治疗中的潜力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A gut bacterium derived from frogs has eliminated tumors in mice, suggesting a microbiome-based approach to cancer therapy. While results are preclinical, the study highlights how manipulating gut microbes could complement existing treatments and prompts further mechanism research.</span><span class='zh'>来自青蛙肠道的细菌在小鼠身上实现肿瘤清除，显示了微生物组干预在癌症治疗中的潜力。尽管尚处于前期实验阶段，该研究凸显了通过调控肠道微生物来辅助现有治疗的可能性，并需要进一步机制研究。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.cloudflare.com/monetization-gateway/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Monetization Gateway: Charge for any resource behind Cloudflare via x402</span><span class='zh'>货币化网关上线</span></a><span class="dom">blog.cloudflare.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cloudflare launches Monetization Gateway, a centralized service to monetize online content through payments, gating, and access control.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 推出 Monetization Gateway，提供一个集中化服务，通过支付、门控和访问控制来实现内容变现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cloudflare launches Monetization Gateway, a centralized service to monetize online content through payments, gating, and access control. It streamlines revenue workflows for publishers, reducing integration friction and enabling more consistent monetization across sites. The product highlights the industry’s move toward turnkey monetization tools for the open web.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 推出 Monetization Gateway，提供一个集中化服务，通过支付、门控和访问控制来实现内容变现。它简化了出版商的收入工作流，降低集成难度，并实现跨站点更一致的变现。该产品凸显了在线内容领域向“端到端”变现工具的趋势。</span></p>

The review underscores robust safety data across diverse populations and highlights potential for rapid development and deployment.</span><span class='zh'>全球综合评估显示 mRNA 疫苗安全、有效，且在应对当前及未来疾病方面前景广阔。评估强调在多样人群中的强健安全性数据，并凸显其在快速开发和部署方面的潜力。</span></p>
