<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ngrok.com/blog/i-ported-kubernetes-to-the-browser" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I ported Kubernetes to the browser</span><span class='zh'>我把 Kubernetes 移植到了浏览器</span></a><span class="dom">ngrok.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Porting Kubernetes to the browser demonstrates running components in WebAssembly or similar in-browser runtimes, enabling offline demos or teaching orchestration concepts.</span><span class='zh'>把 Kubernetes 移植到浏览器演示了在浏览器运行组件的可能性，便于离线演示和教学编排概念。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Porting Kubernetes to the browser demonstrates running components in WebAssembly or similar in-browser runtimes, enabling offline demos or teaching orchestration concepts. It surfaces challenges around networking, storage, and security when moving cluster infrastructure into the client.</span><span class='zh'>把 Kubernetes 移植到浏览器演示了在浏览器运行组件的可能性，便于离线演示和教学编排概念。也暴露了将集群基础设施移至客户端时在网络、存储和安全方面的挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reuters.com/world/china/alibaba-ban-claude-code-workplace-over-alleged-backdoor-risks-source-says-2026-07-03/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Alibaba to ban Claude Code in workplace over alleged backdoor risks, source says</span><span class='zh'>阿里巴巴拟在工作场所禁用 Claude Code，称存在后门风险</span></a><span class="dom">reuters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Alibaba reportedly banned Claude Code in workplace use over alleged backdoor risks, signaling growing caution around enterprise tools.</span><span class='zh'>阿里巴巴据称因后门风险，在工作场所禁用 Claude Code，凸显企业在 AI 编码工具上的风险管理日益严格。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Alibaba reportedly banned Claude Code in workplace use over alleged backdoor risks, signaling growing caution around enterprise tools. This move could influence how other large employers approach AI coding assistants.</span><span class='zh'>阿里巴巴据称因后门风险，在工作场所禁用 Claude Code，凸显企业在 AI 编码工具上的风险管理日益严格。这一举措可能影响其他企业对 AI 编码助手的采用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://unix.foo/posts/last-people-who-know-how-it-works/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We Are the Last People Who Know How It Works</span><span class='zh'>我们是最后一个知道它如何运作的人</span></a><span class="dom">unix.foo</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This piece argues that increasing complexity in software and systems means only a shrinking circle truly understands how things work.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章认为，软件与系统日益复杂，真正懂得运作原理的人越来越少，依赖隐性知识使基础设施变得脆弱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This piece argues that increasing complexity in software and systems means only a shrinking circle truly understands how things work. It warns about fragile infrastructure built on tacit knowledge and advocates better documentation, tooling, and knowledge sharing to reduce risk. The takeaway: preserve institutional memory before it&#x27;s lost.</span><span class='zh'>这篇文章认为，软件与系统日益复杂，真正懂得运作原理的人越来越少，依赖隐性知识使基础设施变得脆弱。作者呼吁加强文档、工具和知识共享，降低运维风险，提醒要在知识流失前留住记忆。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Knoppix, the pioneer live Linux distribution, is surveyed—its approach to hardware autodetection and running from removable media influenced modern portability and recovery workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Knoppix 作为开创性实时/现场运行的 Linux 发行版的概览，展示其硬件自检测与从可移动介质启动的理念如何影响现代便携与救援场景。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Knoppix, the pioneer live Linux distribution, is surveyed—its approach to hardware autodetection and running from removable media influenced modern portability and recovery workflows. The piece traces why Knoppix mattered for the era of live CDs and USB booting. It offers context for current portable Linux tools.</span><span class='zh'>Knoppix 作为开创性实时/现场运行的 Linux 发行版的概览，展示其硬件自检测与从可移动介质启动的理念如何影响现代便携与救援场景。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/74066" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Potential session/cache leakage between workspace instances or consumer accounts</span><span class='zh'>工作区实例或消费账户间的会话/缓存泄漏潜在风险</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A bug report highlights potential session or cache leakage across Claude Code workspace instances or consumer accounts, threatening data isolation in multi-tenant deployments.</span><span class='zh'>关于 Claude Code 的工作区实例之间或消费账户之间可能出现会话/缓存泄漏的问题报道，暴露多租户环境的数据隔离风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A bug report highlights potential session or cache leakage across Claude Code workspace instances or consumer accounts, threatening data isolation in multi-tenant deployments. If real, it could let one user access another&#x27;s session data or cached prompts. Mitigations focus on stricter tenant isolation, scoped caching, shorter TTLs, and robust audit trails.</span><span class='zh'>关于 Claude Code 的工作区实例之间或消费账户之间可能出现会话/缓存泄漏的问题报道，暴露多租户环境的数据隔离风险。若属实，可能让一个用户访问到另一用户的会话数据或缓存提示。应对措施包括更严格的租户隔离、缓存作用域控制、缩短生存期以及完善的审计日志。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>311</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48785485' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>129</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48772573" data-id="48772573" data-tags="Programming|Web" data-score="307" data-comments="102" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48772573" data-time="1783068647">

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WordGard is a new in-browser rich-text editor by the ProseMirror creator, aiming to streamline building modern editing experiences with collaboration and formatting features.</span><span class='zh'>WordGard 是 ProseMirror 作者推出的全新浏览器端富文本编辑器，旨在简化现代协作与排版功能的编辑体验开发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WordGard is a new in-browser rich-text editor by the ProseMirror creator, aiming to streamline building modern editing experiences with collaboration and formatting features. It promises a modular architecture and ergonomic APIs that could accelerate web-based content tools.</span><span class='zh'>WordGard 是 ProseMirror 作者推出的全新浏览器端富文本编辑器，旨在简化现代协作与排版功能的编辑体验开发。它承诺模块化架构与易用 API，可能加速网页内容工具的开发。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/teamchong/pxpipe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>60% Fable cost cut by converting code to images and having the model OCR it</span><span class='zh'>通过将代码转换为图像并让模型进行 OCR，Fable 成本降低 60%</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A cost-cutting experiment shows that encoding code as images and using OCR can dramatically reduce token usage in Fable, cutting costs by about 60%.</span><span class='zh'>在 Fable 的成本优化尝试中，將代码转成图像再用模型 OCR，大约降低 60% 的成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A cost-cutting experiment shows that encoding code as images and using OCR can dramatically reduce token usage in Fable, cutting costs by about 60%. The approach highlights an unusual, input-format hack to optimize model usage, with trade-offs in accuracy and workflow.</span><span class='zh'>在 Fable 的成本优化尝试中，將代码转成图像再用模型 OCR，大约降低 60% 的成本。这一做法揭示了通过输入形式的创新来降低模型成本的潜在可能性，同时也伴随准确性和工作流的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/google/copybara" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google copybara: moving code between repositories</span><span class='zh'>Google Copybara：跨代码库迁移代码的工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Copybara automates code migration across repositories with customizable transformations and history preservation.</span><span class='zh'>Copybara 能在不同代码库之间自动迁移代码，支持自定义转换并保留提交历史。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Copybara automates code migration across repositories with customizable transformations and history preservation. It helps teams maintain consistent workflows when moving assets, enabling cleaner separation of projects and easier collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>Copybara 能在不同代码库之间自动迁移代码，支持自定义转换并保留提交历史。它帮助团队在跨仓库迁移时保持工作流的一致性，使项目分离更清晰、协作更高效。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/searxng/searxng" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SearXNG: A free internet metasearch engine</span><span class='zh'>SearXNG：一个免费的互联网元搜索引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SearXNG is a free, open source metasearch engine that aggregates results from multiple sources while preserving user privacy.</span><span class='zh'>SearXNG 是一个免费且开源的元搜索引擎，汇聚多家源端结果并强调用户隐私。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SearXNG is a free, open source metasearch engine that aggregates results from multiple sources while preserving user privacy. It is a modern fork with improved privacy defaults and straightforward self hosting, appealing to privacy minded researchers and admins.</span><span class='zh'>SearXNG 是一个免费且开源的元搜索引擎，汇聚多家源端结果并强调用户隐私。作为现代分支，它提供更严格的隐私默认和便于自托管的架构，适合注重隐私的研究者与系统管理员。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/deepreinforce-ai/Ornith-1" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ornith-1.0: self-improving open-source models for agentic coding</span><span class='zh'>Ornith-1.0：具备代理能力的自我改进开源模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ornith-1.0 pushes open-source models toward self-improvement for agentic coding.</span><span class='zh'>Ornith-1.0 将开源模型推向自我改进，聚焦具备代理能力的编码任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ornith-1.0 pushes open-source models toward self-improvement for agentic coding. The project explores mechanisms for models to refine their behavior and tooling autonomously, reducing human-labeled overhead. If successful, it could accelerate autonomous programming tasks and bring more capable AI-assisted development to the community.</span><span class='zh'>Ornith-1.0 将开源模型推向自我改进，聚焦具备代理能力的编码任务。它尝试让模型自主优化行为和工具使用，降低人工标注的开销。一旦成熟，可能显著加速自动化编程和 AI 辅助开发的能力落地。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openai/codex/issues/2847" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A way to exclude sensitive files issue still open for OpenAI Codex</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI Codex 的排除敏感文件方法仍未解决</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An open issue around how to exclude sensitive files from Codex training or generation remains unresolved.</span><span class='zh'>关于如何从 Codex 的训练或输出中排除敏感文件的讨论仍未有定论，尚处于未解决状态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An open issue around how to exclude sensitive files from Codex training or generation remains unresolved. The discussion highlights ongoing data governance and privacy challenges in deploying code-generating AI, where guarding private or proprietary content is critical.</span><span class='zh'>关于如何从 Codex 的训练或输出中排除敏感文件的讨论仍未有定论，尚处于未解决状态。此议题凸显了代码生成模型在数据治理和隐私保护方面的持续挑战，以及对私有或机密内容的防护需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ogulcancelik/herdr" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Herdr: Agent multiplexer that lives in your terminal</span><span class='zh'>Herdr：驻留在终端中的代理多路复用器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Herdr is a terminal-based agent multiplexer that lets you run and coordinate multiple agents in a single shell session.</span><span class='zh'>Herdr 是一个在终端运行的代理多路复用器，允许在一个 shell 会话中同时管理和协同多个代理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Herdr is a terminal-based agent multiplexer that lets you run and coordinate multiple agents in a single shell session. It enables modular agent orchestration with shared I/O, reducing context switching for power users.</span><span class='zh'>Herdr 是一个在终端运行的代理多路复用器，允许在一个 shell 会话中同时管理和协同多个代理。它支持模块化编排、共享输入输出，降低高强度用户的上下文切换成本。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bramble is a local first password manager that prioritizes offline storage and privacy, with optional sync that preserves user control.</span><span class='zh'>Bramble 作为本地优先的密码管理器，强调离线存储与隐私保护，支持可控的同步。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bramble is a local first password manager that prioritizes offline storage and privacy, with optional sync that preserves user control. The Show HN signals a compelling alternative to cloud centric password managers and invites discussion on threat models.</span><span class='zh'>Bramble 作为本地优先的密码管理器，强调离线存储与隐私保护，支持可控的同步。Show HN 的展示凸显了对比云端为主的密码管理方案的潜在优势，并引发关于威胁模型的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jumpserver/jumpserver" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>JumpServer: Open-Source Privileged Access Management</span><span class='zh'>JumpServer：开源特权访问管理工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>JumpServer emerges as an open-source Privileged Access Management solution.</span><span class='zh'>JumpServer 作为开源特权访问管理解决方案渐入人心，提供集中访问控制、会话记录与审计等能力，帮助关键系统的安全管控。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>JumpServer emerges as an open-source Privileged Access Management solution. It offers centralized access control, session recording, and auditing for critical systems, presenting a cost-effective alternative to proprietary PAM. For security teams, it lowers barriers to strong PAM practices and integration with existing infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>JumpServer 作为开源特权访问管理解决方案渐入人心，提供集中访问控制、会话记录与审计等能力，帮助关键系统的安全管控。对安全团队而言，它提供了一更具性价比的替代方案，易于与现有基础设施整合。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/pIat0n/BareMetal-RAM-Dumper" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>BareMetal RAM Dumper – Bare-metal x86 tool for Cold Boot Attack experiments</span><span class='zh'>BareMetal RAM Dumper：用于冷启动攻击实验的 Bare-metal x86 工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A bare-metal RAM dumper enables memory dumps during cold-boot attack demonstrations, useful for hardware security researchers exploring memory residency and data remnants.</span><span class='zh'>BareMetal RAM Dumper 是一款在裸机上执行内存转储的工具，便于在冷启动攻击演示中提取内存数据，帮助研究者研究内存留存与敏感信息泄露。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A bare-metal RAM dumper enables memory dumps during cold-boot attack demonstrations, useful for hardware security researchers exploring memory residency and data remnants. The tool lowers the barrier to reproduce classic attack scenarios on x86 hardware, but underscores ethics and legal boundaries in performing such experiments.</span><span class='zh'>BareMetal RAM Dumper 是一款在裸机上执行内存转储的工具，便于在冷启动攻击演示中提取内存数据，帮助研究者研究内存留存与敏感信息泄露。该工具降低了在 x86 硬件上复现传统攻击情景的门槛，但同时必须遵循伦理和法律边界。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DNSGlobe is a Rust-based TUI that visualizes DNS propagation around the world, offering a lightweight tool for operators to observe latency, regional differences, and caching behavior. It demonstrates how terminal apps can serve practical network insight without heavy dashboards.</span><span class='zh'>DNSGlobe 是基于 Rust 的 TUI 工具，用以可视化全球范围内的 DNS 传播情况，方便运维观察延迟和区域差异。展示了在运维场景中，文本界面也能提供实用的网络洞察。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ClickHouse/RayTracer" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ray Tracer in SQL</span><span class='zh'>SQL 实现的光线追踪</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ClickHouse&#x27;s Ray Tracer in SQL demonstrates how expressive SQL can be when used for graphics-like workloads, implementing ray tracing entirely in SQL primitives.</span><span class='zh'>ClickHouse 将光线追踪完全用 SQL 原语实现，展示了 SQL 在图形化工作负载中的表达力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ClickHouse&#x27;s Ray Tracer in SQL demonstrates how expressive SQL can be when used for graphics-like workloads, implementing ray tracing entirely in SQL primitives. The project serves as a provocative proof of concept and a pointer to what’s possible with vectorized engines. It invites exploration of SQL-based rendering techniques and performance considerations.</span><span class='zh'>ClickHouse 将光线追踪完全用 SQL 原语实现，展示了 SQL 在图形化工作负载中的表达力。该项目是一种挑衅性的概念证明，指向向量化引擎潜在的可能性，同时促使人们探讨基于 SQL 的渲染技术及其性能边界。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pglayers treats PostgreSQL extensions as stackable Docker layers to compose images modularly, speeding up deployment and enabling reuse across projects.</span><span class='zh'>Pglayers 将 PostgreSQL 扩展视为可堆叠的 Docker 层，以模块化组合镜像，提升部署速度与跨项目复用性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pglayers treats PostgreSQL extensions as stackable Docker layers to compose images modularly, speeding up deployment and enabling reuse across projects.</span><span class='zh'>Pglayers 将 PostgreSQL 扩展视为可堆叠的 Docker 层，以模块化组合镜像，提升部署速度与跨项目复用性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/adam-s/car-diagnosis" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Classify mechanical faults using Contrastive Language-Audio Pretraining</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用对比语言-音频预训练来分类机械故障</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A lightweight demonstration shows how Contrastive Language-Audio Pretraining (CLAP) can classify mechanical faults from audio signals using simple prompts.</span><span class='zh'>一个简短的示例展示利用对比语言-音频预训练（CLAP）从声音信号中分类机械故障，并通过简单提示实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A lightweight demonstration shows how Contrastive Language-Audio Pretraining (CLAP) can classify mechanical faults from audio signals using simple prompts. It highlights a practical path toward data-efficient fault diagnosis that can reduce downtime and maintenance costs.</span><span class='zh'>一个简短的示例展示利用对比语言-音频预训练（CLAP）从声音信号中分类机械故障，并通过简单提示实现。该方案体现了数据高效的故障诊断潜力，有望降低停机时间与维护成本。</span></p>
